# UI 0.5.0 public API review

Complete reviewed contract inventory. KEEP names remain supported; REMOVE names retire in 0.5.0. Runtime and packed compiler qualification are enforced by the repository gates.

Inventory source: `cab46b0ed82c3c2fc9e974e1d7b3c828bba57be6`; implementation base: `311a978328c6174cfca9ae11139c7f9b48c4f632` after the Core testing consumer prerequisite. The published API inventory is identical at both heads. The contract changes 970→864 name/path pairs (864 KEEP, 106 REMOVE), and 485→432 unique names. All 36 export keys and 127 component values remain. The 27 accessibility constants and 26 metadata type aliases retire at both root and owning family paths. See [migration guidance](./migration-0.5.0.md).

## Package conditions

All 36 keys remain. Each of the 35 runtime entrypoints retains both `import`
and `require`, resolving to the same ESM `.js` file and `.d.ts` declaration.
Native Node 24 `require(esm)` avoids the old duplicate `.cjs` graph that failed
on the ESM-only Core peer. This package repair is separate from the 970→864
named-pair count. See the migration guide for supported consumer syntax.

## Complete name/path decisions

| Path                  | Name                                  | Decision | Concrete consumer reason                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | Migration                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | -------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `.`                   | `Accordion`                           | KEEP     | Application composition owns single/multiple disclosure state and coordinates item values, trigger navigation and content visibility. The documented multi-family root is used by Themes, CLI starters, Examples, Website and Destroyer; retain this deliberate aggregate composition contract alongside the narrower family import.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `.`                   | `ACCORDION_A11Y_CONTRACT`             | REMOVE   | Accessibility assertion metadata is a repository-owned documentation/test fixture, not a component runtime capability or application input contract. Keep the .a11y implementation fixture available to relative tests and preserve its behavioral assertions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Use documented component roles, ARIA/data attributes and public behavior tests for application assertions. Keep any app-specific expected contract local; there is no replacement runtime export or new testing subpath. |
| `.`                   | `AccordionA11yContract`               | REMOVE   | This typeof-accessibility-fixture alias only names repository-owned assertion metadata; it is not component props or an application input/result contract.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | Use documented component roles, ARIA/data attributes and public behavior tests for application assertions. Keep any app-specific expected contract local; there is no replacement runtime export or new testing subpath. |
| `.`                   | `AccordionContent`                    | KEEP     | Application composition renders the family content host with the owning root context, visibility and accessibility associations (AccordionContent).
